- 1 - 
個股報告

順德(2351) 
功率第一、均熱突圍，電源與散熱雙引擎同時引爆 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $410 
前次投資建議：2026.08.10 
「買進」 ，目標價: $252

報告日期：2026.09.10 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求回溫，均熱片新案發酵，2026 年將重返榮耀，EPS 7.4元 
➢ AI 產品全面放量，均熱片進度優於預期，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利倍增，上修 EPS 至
15.2 元，考量到今年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強勁，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價調
升至 410 元(27X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 72.6%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/9/9 收盤價： 237.50 ➢ 展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自  AI 產品線，
目前逾 60 支新產品開案，在擴產效益帶動下，預期今
年 AI 相關營收倍數增長；均熱片近期已通過 4 間大
客戶認證，新增 4 台高噸位沖床與 15 台 CNC，並
年底達 100 萬片月產能、明年中達 2,000 萬片目標，
惟現階段量小、今年挹注不大。毛利率受惠  26H1 漲
價、稼動率高檔與產品組合轉佳，加上下半年客戶加價
搶產能，上修預估。綜上，兆豐投顧上修 2026年稅後
淨利 14.4 億元，YoY +374.4%，EPS 7.4 元。 
➢ 展望 2027 年，隨單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 
以上，產業加速朝 HVDC 架構演進，順德憑藉與歐美
大廠合作開發的高功率、高散熱、高可靠度導線架成為
主要受惠者 ， AI 導線架營收可望隨  ± 400V/800V 
HVDC 滲透率提升而續強；均熱片進度優於研究部預
期，2,000 萬片有機會於 27 年中到位，隨 NPI 專案
逐步落地，27H2 動能將顯著增長，並隨 CPU/GPU 大
尺寸擴張成為新一輪成長主軸。在組合優化與稼動率維
持高檔下，毛利率可望續揚，兆豐投顧上修順德 2027 
年營收 206.4 億元(原估 155 億元)，YoY +45.7%，
毛利率 24.1%(原估 21.3%)，年增 2.8ppt，稅後淨利 
29.4 億元，YoY +104.5%，EPS 15.2 元(原估 8.4 
元)，考量到今 明年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強
勁，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價調升至410元(27x 
FY27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間72.6%。

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順德 2025年跨入 AI server領域，2026年散熱領域正式發酵 
順德(2351)成立於 1967 年，以辦公文具起家，1983 年跨入半導體導線架，
並於 2010 年與日本三菱伸銅(mitsubishi-shindoh)技術合作，取得導線架原
料銅合金製程設備及技術授權，目前產品涵蓋 IC 導線架、功率/分離式元件
導線架、LED 導線架、音圈馬達彈片，終端應用遍及車用、工業與消費性電
子。順德目前在全球導線架市佔排名前五，而在 Power IC 導線架則排名第
一。順德過往多以車用產品為主，不過隨著資料中心電源架構的改變，順德
與英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管理經驗移轉至資料中心，順德成為全台唯一 AI 
server 導線架主力供應商，並於 2025 年開始量產高功率導線架與穩壓器。
而順德自 2024 年布局均熱片跨足散熱領域，並於 2026 年開始生產 CPO 
Switch產品，而多家 CSP 之 CPU與 GPU 也有望於本年度驗證後開始出貨，
預期均熱片月產能從 26年底 100萬片大幅拉升至 27年 2,000萬片(不含 NPI
產品需求規劃)，反映 AI導線架與均熱片需求大幅增長，順德於中國江蘇廠、
台灣彰化廠與南投廠同步擴產，並新購大噸位沖床，同時也在台灣與馬來西
亞找地新建廠房以因應未來幾年高壓電源與均熱片之高速成長期。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
AI 導線架供不應求，輔以銅價上漲與客戶加價搶購，上修 26Q3 EPS至 2.3 
元 
順德(2351)公告 8月營收：營收 13.3 億元，MoM -0.1%/YoY +61.2%，為單
月次高，Q3達成率 69%，遠優研究部預期，未能再創高係因消費性包括 VCM
與文具類產品較上月有所衰退，不過預期在車用需求全面復甦與 AI 導線架
供不應求，Q3營收可望再創高至 36.5 億元，QoQ +3%/YoY +43.6%，車
用方面：除歐美車廠需求延續外，陸系新品也將推出，預期車用成長動能將
延續，此外 AI導線架於 Q2有近 30%季增長，預期在新產能開出下，營收將
逐月創高，而在產品組合優化與稼動率提升下，同時 AI 導線架供不應求使客
戶加價搶產能，故上修毛利率至 22%(原估 20%)，季增1.3ppt，年增9.6ppt，
同步上修稅後淨利 4.4 億元，QoQ +45.6%/YoY +405.2%，EPS 2.3 元 (原
估 1.8元)。

AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求明顯回溫，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將
重返榮耀，EPS 7.4元 
展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自 AI 產品線，隨著伺服器電源架構改
變，PSU 瓦數持續提升，高功率導線架需求將被徹底激發，從高壓電網、
SST(固態變壓器)、Power Stage、48V bus converter、800V HVDC 與 BBU 
皆須用到高功率導線架，而順德自 2024 年攜手英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管
理經驗移轉至資料中心 ，使順德成為全球  AI server 導線架主力供應商之
一，目前有超過 60 支新產品開案，同時在擴產效益帶動下，預期 2026 年
AI 相關營收將倍數增長；在車用方面，受惠庫存調整結束，同時電動車需求
激增與新車款推出，預期全年呈雙位數增長；在均熱片方面，公司耕耘近兩
年，近期已通過 4 間大客戶認證，除新增 4 台高噸位沖床外也同步增加 15
台 CNC 設備，同時也於近期租賃新廠房以滿足年底 100 萬片月產能需求，
並於明年中達成 2,000 萬片產能目標，公司放眼價值更高 NPI，研究部認為
產業最具指標之共平面度，順德已能做至 30μm 以下，名列產業前茅，預期
將受客戶大幅青睞，更大尺寸產品用於 ASIC/CPU/GPU 均熱片有望陸續驗
證出貨，惟現階段出貨量較小且產能有限，預期今年對營運挹注不大。毛利
率方面：受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，同時產品組合轉佳下，且
下半年客戶加價搶產能 ，上修毛利率預估。綜上所述，兆豐投顧上修順德  
2026 年營收 142 億元，YoY +3 8.3%，毛利率 21.3%，年增 8ppt，稅後
淨利 14.4 億元，YoY +374.4%，EPS 7.4元。

AI 產品全面放量，均熱片進度優於預期，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利倍增，
EPS 15.2 元 
展望 2027 年，隨著單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 以上，過往 48V/54V 
供電架構已不敷使用，故產業正加速朝 HVDC(高壓直流)架構演進，順德憑
藉與歐美大廠合作開發的高功率、高散熱、高可靠度的導線架，將成為主要

- 4 - 
個股報告 
受惠者，預期在±400V/800V HVDC 架構滲透率提升帶動 AI導線架營收持續
高速增長；在均熱片方面，順德進度優於研究部預期，2,000 萬片有機會於
27年中到位，同時隨著 NPI專案逐步落地，27H2 營運動能將有顯著增長，
研究部認為均熱片可望隨著 CPU/GPU 更大尺寸擴張，成為新一輪成長主
軸；車用則在電動化與在地化趨勢下維持穩健成長。在產品組合優化與稼動
率維持高檔下，毛利率可望續揚，兆豐投顧上修順德 2027 年營收 206.4 億
元(原估 155 億元)，YoY +45.7%，毛利率24.1%(原估 21.3%)，年增2.8ppt，
稅後淨利 29.4 億元，YoY +104.5%，EPS 15.2 元(原估 8.4 元)。

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,750 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 27.5x for 2H26-1H27E, which we derive from the mid range of
prior upcycle valuation 25-35x during 2021 in view of a strong upcycle with solid FA demand and ongoing share gains.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China

macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;
and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Elite Material
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$6,000, based on a target PE multiple of 30x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our
target PE multiple, at the peak of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings outlook supported
by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC (high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec

upgrade for CCL and price hikes should help support its margin expansion going forward. At our target price, the shares would
trade at 23.6x/13.2x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which we largely attribute to frequent debates over the
spec upgrade for next-gen AI servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope for aggressive spec
upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in

the end. However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth
potential among AI servers of different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL
upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg, glass, copper foil,

foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 1

•Broad-base 2Q, cost-capped margin. Mgmt. reiterated that 2Q26 growth 
was broad-based across every product line, end market and region, not a 
single AI pocket. GPM was slightly below Street, as raw material cost 
inflation offset part of the pricing and mix improvement. Internal 
manufacturing efficiency projects are underway to absorb it in the future.
•Jul price hike hits spot and direct. The Jul price adjustment covers spot 
prices and direct customers, vs. earlier rounds limited to contract 
customers, and spans c.50% of revenue including MLCC, tantalum and 
other capacitors. Spot pricing flows through immediately rather than at 
renewal, so mgmt. expects ASP and revenue effects shortly, with margin 
impact far more visible in 2H. Mgmt. also sees no sign of demand slowing 
down in the near-term.
•Tantalum now the largest line. AI is not an MLCC-only story for passive 
components. Tantalum grew fastest of any line in 2Q, suits AI power 
management, and has over taken magnetics to become Yageo's largest 
product line. MLCC also grew well and should keep growing in coming 
quarters. AI revenue is now 16% of total revenue, spread across tantalums, 
MLCCs, resistors and inductors rather than single product.
•65 sites, dual-region by product. Every product is manufactured in at least 
two regions, giving customers flexibility against tariff and geopolitical risks. 
Mgmt. believe the existing 65 facilities are flexible in adding new lines at a 
fast pace whenever shortages appear, saving customers 1-2 years vs. 
building from greenfield. The company also planned the capacity expansion 
ahead of demand.

### Page 2

Yageo (2327.TW)
04 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•YFIII and overseas MLCC bases. YFIII in Kaohsiung, commissioned in 2024, is Yageo's largest MLCC fab and still has
space for additional MLCC lines, with AI-specific equipment arriving monthly since last year. Outside Taiwan, Yageo
has been opening new lines within existing facilities in Mexico and China, leaving it able to absorb incremental AI
orders without a new building cycle.
•Lead times extend, LTA interest rises. Lead time of most of the products has lengthened vs. 1H26. Tantalum is the

tightest, followed by MLCC and resistors. Mgmt. indicated all AI-related lines are extremely tight, and more
customers are now approaching Yageo to sign LTAs.
•NP0 leadership on the power side. Yageo estimates a meaningful share (at least 30-40%) of NP0 high-voltage
MLCC, based on the internal assessment. Built on EV and industrial experience, NP0 fits AI's high-voltage, high-
temp duty and sits on the DC power side, while high-cap MLCC serves the near-chip stage. Yageo ships direct to

leading power supply players and is invited as first batch of design partners.
•Low-to-high conversion only partial. Converting low-end MLCC capacity to high-end is only partially possible.
High-spec MLCC stores more capacitance through higher layer counts, requiring advanced stackers rather than
conventional equipment. Yageo can optimize and redeploy some existing tooling, but not fully substitute, which is
why it is committing capex to new AI production line instead of relying on conversion.

### Page 3

Yageo (2327.TW)

3
Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.

after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 3

Earnings revision: We factor in VPEC’s Jul-Aug revenues and raise our 2026-28E net 
incomes by 12% / 18% / 30%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. 2026-28E revenues 
are revised up by 7% / 14% / 25%, mainly on higher contribution from optoelectronics 
business, driven by the strong end demand of AI data center optical networking, better 
InP substrate supply, and VPEC’s customers’ expansion and product expansion from CW 
lasers to EML, along with specification upgrade. Our 2026-28E GMs are revised up on 
rising contribution from optoelectronics business, which carries a higher margin than 
other product lines. We revise up 2027-28E opex ratios, mainly on higher R&D expense 
to collaborate with key customers on next generation optical networking products.

### Page 8

VPEC focuses on III-V compound semiconductor EPI wafer for microelectronics and 
optoelectronics devices based on InP and GaA substrate, and expands to EPI wafer for 
SiPh transceivers CW laser for data center applications. We expect strong momentum in 
VPEC growth driven by the SiPh ramp up in 2026E, microelectronics clients’ increasing 
demand, and its accumulated technology advantages on EPI wafer. We note that VPEC is 
currently trading lower than our TP implied 69x 2027E forward P/E, which is attractive, 
in our view.  
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC 
Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s 
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6% 
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is 
based on 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics 
supply chain. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected 
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Update
Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
August Sales +4% MoM/+59%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Accton's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• August sales were NT$40,873mn (+4% MoM/+59% YoY).
• QTD reached 66% of our 3Q26 estimate, at NT$120,883mn (+27% QoQ/
+66% YoY), and 70% of consensus, at NT$114,321mn (+20% QoQ/+57%
YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 revenue tracking in line with our estimates

• Our checks indicate that Accton has been negotiating with customers to let
them consign  those expensive components, which could weigh on its
revenue growth but should be positive to margins.
• Looking ahead, we expect the momentum to remain elevated into 2H26, as it
should benefit from broader-based migration to 800G network switches
from both existing and new customers, and the ramp of the new AI
accelerator model from its major customer should be in full swing.

• Stay OW with a PT of NT$3,500, based on 30x 2027 P/E.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for

an operating profit CAGR of 32%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger data center capex
n Faster network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand losing share in AI networks
n Stronger custom AI accelerator growth
n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside
n Weaker data center capex
n Slower network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
n Softer custom AI accelerator growth
n Softer ODM direct business model adoption

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales  +4% MoM/+156%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Chroma's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• August revenue was NT$5,632mn (+4% MoM/+156% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 75% of MSe, at NT$14,772mn (+9% QoQ/+130% YoY),
and 79% of consensus, at NT$13,992mn (+3% QoQ/+118% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 tracking ahead of market expectations and likely to deliver

over 20% QoQ.
• Looking ahead, we expect its power testing business to stay at an elevated
level, and the semiconductor & photonics business to continue growing HoH
into 2H26, driven by SLT, metrology, CPO and potential shipment of FT
handlers/burn-in ovens.
• Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
TFT-LCD Panel Price Outlook
for Sep-26: TVs, Monitors and
NBs Flat MoM
Panel pricing should stat flat for major applications in the
coming months. We believe that the glass opportunities in
advanced packaging are already in the price for panel makers,
though the timing for mass production and intensifying
competition could be potential risk factors.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Derrick Yang

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
S. Korea Technology
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive

75" 4K 65" 4K 55" FHD 50" FHD 43" FHD 32" HD
Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices to stay flat MoM in September: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and
75" TV panel prices are all expected to stay flat for the month of September. This is
consistent with our expectations. On the demand side, although overall demand is
lackluster, there is still some seasonal restocking demand from brand customers to
prepare for  year-end promotion campaigns. On the supply side, panel makers remain

disciplined on output control and plan some annual maintenance to adjust
utilization during the coming October holidays in China. On a quarterly average
basis, we expect TV panel prices to decline by low-single-digit % QoQ in 3Q26.
IT panel prices to stay flat MoM in  September: Monitor and notebook panel prices
are expected to stay flat MoM during the month. Both applications are  seeing
slower demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about

increasing component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and
reluctant to given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect
IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 3Q26.
Risk/reward less attractive: We think the current valuation for Taiwanese panel
names AUO (2409.TW; 1.5x P/B) and Innolux (3481.TW; 1.7x P/B) have already baked
in some expectations of glass in advanced packaging starting to make revenue
contribution in 2028. Nonetheless, some supply chain players think this might take

longer, and there seem to be many suppliers from different supply chains vying for
this opportunity as well, which could be a potential risk to monitor. In China, we
prefer BOE (000725.SZ; 1.4x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B), on its scale
advantage and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Seasonal restocking along with disciplined output control should help stabilize
TV panel prices in the coming months.
IT panel prices are expected to stay roughly flat in coming quarters as well.
Potential opportunities for glass in advanced packaging seem  already  priced in,
with the risk in commercialization timing and competition worth monitoring.

September 8, 2026 12:04 AM GMT

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trending Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM and August was down 1% MoM, in line with our expectation that
TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That said, we do not expect a
significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide downside support.

Below-Seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization To Be at ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance

following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.
Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, while we expect a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing

structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to
profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Fair After Recent Correction
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display, and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.7x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end-demand: If end-demand for major applications turns out to be
better than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-09
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: Eternal Precision Mechanics (EPM) reported its August 2026 
revenue after market hours on 9 September 2026.

What’s the impact: August revenue beat expectations. EPM reported 
August 2026 revenue of TWD359.8m, up 95.7% MoM and 111.5% YoY. 
QTD revenue reached 88.4% of our previous 3Q26 forecast and 74.2% of 
the Bloomberg consensus. Given the faster-than-expected revenue 
recognition schedule and a sharp increase in recognised high-end 
equipment units, which should drive a more favourable product mix, we 
raise our 3Q26 revenue forecast by 33% to TWD972.7m, gross margin by 
3.1ppts to 48.5%, and EPS by 92% to TWD1.86.

Vacuum laminators are emerging as the next ABF bottleneck. Despite 
a partial conversion of backlog into revenue in August, EPM’s backlog 
continued to increase from TWD810m at end-July to TWD880m in early 
September. With lead times extending further to 36 months, vacuum 
laminators are emerging as the next bottleneck in the ABF capacity 
expansion cycle. Given the robust demand from ABF substrate makers, we 
see upside potential to EPM’s capacity expansion plans. On margins, we 
expect continued product-mix improvement and the full realisation of price 
increases to lift gross margin by 6.6ppts/1.3ppts YoY to 53.2%/54.5% in 
2027/28E, driving a 2026-28E EPS CAGR of 141%.

Moulding film adoption opens a new growth avenue. Traditional 
transfer and compression moulding typically use granular, powder, or liquid-
type compounds for encapsulation. During our visit to SEMICON, Japanese 
material suppliers Nagase (8012 JP, JPY1,150.5, Outperform [2]; covered 
by Masayuki Nagano) and Resonac (4004 JP, JPY16,070, Outperform [2]; 
covered by Hidemitsu Umebayashi) noted growing customer interest in 
moulding films. These offer better filling performance, better warpage 
control and higher flowability. This makes them increasingly suitable for 
advanced packaging applications such as panel level packaging and 
component-embedded substrates. EPM supplies moulding-film laminators 
for both applications, with revenue contribution expected to begin in 2027.

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 22-26% to reflect the 
full realisation of price increases and improving product mix. We reaffirm 
our Buy (1) rating and raise our 12-month TP to TWD720 (from TWD600) 
based on an unchanged target PER of 40x, applied to our 1-year forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: slower-than-expected substrate capex 
growth.

How we differ: We likely conduct a more comprehensive analysis than 
others on ABF industry demand and moulding film development.

9 September 2026

Vacuum laminators: the next ABF bottleneck

➢ 3Q26 QTD revenue ahead on higher high-end equipment recognition 
➢ Product mix and price hikes drive margins to new highs 
➢ Reaffirming Buy (1) rating; lifting 12M TP to TWD720

Company profile 
Eternal was established in 2022 through the spin-off of Eternal Material’s equipment division and its 
subsequent merger with Nikko-Materials, a wholly owned Japanese subsidiary. The company was listed 
in January 2026 and is the global leader in IC substrate vacuum laminators, serving all major substrate 
manufacturers worldwide.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 26 Jun 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Eternal demonstrates a solid corporate governance structure supported by a balanced and
diversified board composition. The company’sboard consists of 7 members, including 3
independent directors, with backgrounds spanning industry operations, finance, accounting,
legal, and academia, enabling comprehensive oversight across business and governance
functions.
Capital management 2
Eternal adopts a prudent capital management strategy, supported by a stable shareholder
structure and disciplined financial policy. The company is majority-owned by Eternal Materials
(over 60% stake), providing long-term strategic backing and financial stability.
Related party &
transaction 1
Eternal discloses related-party transactions transparently and enforces strict governance
procedures to manage potential conflicts of interest. Board members are required to recuse
themselves from discussions and voting on matters involving personal or affiliated interests,
such as asset acquisitions, patent licensing, and remuneration decisions.
S Product quality &
safety 1
Eternal maintains strict product quality control, covering design, manufacturing, and delivery
processes. The company emphasizes equipment reliability and performance stability, supported
by long-term client relationships and low defect risks, ensuring high customer satisfaction and
consistent product safety standards.
E Product design &
lifecycle management 2
Eternal focuses on enhancing product durability and energy efficiency through continuous R&D
innovation. Its equipment design emphasizes precision, extended lifecycle and reduced material
waste, aligning product development with resource efficiency and long-term environmental
sustainability goals.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max: (1) Cooling: the new iPhone is embedded with a more 
powerful SoC (A20 Pro) and Apple Intelligence, making thermal management essential 
to the device. The next generation of Vapor Chamber is used in the new iPhones, which 
we see as positive for the cooling suppliers. Read more: AVC, AAC; (2) Camera: variable 
aperture is adopted in rear cameras, in which lens aperture changes based on the focal 
length, allowing easier user control of DoF (depth of field), taking pictures in low lighting, 
or having a more natural broken gradient background when in portrait mode. Read 
more: Largan. Selling price is higher by US$100 (or high single-digit %) compared to 
iPhone 17 Pro series, with the same delivery time.  
Apple Watch and Airpods: Airpods 5 feature active noise cancellation, and are 
designed to allow more natural interaction (e.g. transparency mode, better conversation 
awareness). The wireless charging case models are US$30 cheaper compared to Airpods 
4. Apple Watch 12 and Ultra 4 are priced the same as the previous generation with 
better specifications: health sensing system with the most accurate heart rate sensing in 
a wearable, Siri recap, audio intelligence, etc.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

estimates.
We expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to be the key
beneficiaries of the new iPhone launch. We downgrade Largan
to EW and Genius to UW.  Both can benefit from the variable
aperture lens upgrade, but enthusiasm over Co-Packaged Optics

has made the stocks overvalued.
The iPhone opportunity is real for Largan and Genius…but their stocks have
rallied 48% and 85%, respectively, in the past month: (The TAIEX is up 5% over
this period.)
We now value Largan at NT$7,100/share – no longer attractive: Our analysis
suggests that Largan's current share price already embeds more than 30% global
market share in Fiber Array Units (FAU) before formal production starts in 2H27 or
even 2028. Our residual income model yields a value of NT$3,000/share for the

company's traditional business, while our TAM analysis derives a value of NT$4,100/
share for CPO/FAU new business, bringing the total price target to NT$7,100.
However, any delay in CPO/FAU development poses significant downside risk. We
therefore downgrade to EW.
We now value Genius at NT$910/share – but  even on this basis, we view it as
overvalued: Similarly, we believe Genius's share price is also inflated by hopes for
FAU components. Using a similar approach, we conclude that aggregate value is NT

$910/share (NT$600 for existing business and NT$310/share for FAU component
business). We recommend taking profits.
Reiterate OW on Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal and AAC: We believe all four companies
will benefit from the new iPhone launch. Here, however,  we still see upside in the
stocks. We see these companies, benefiting in various ways:
• Luxshare: Key player in iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max assembly, also benefits from
variable aperture upgrade as component supplier.

•  Lingyi: Higher content value form both iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and foldable.
•  Crystal: Micro prism modules, coated filters, and cover lenses should enjoy
positive ASP and volume growth.
• AAC: Vapor chamber (VC) heat dissipation upgrade for iPhone 18 Pro Max
and Pro is the key catalyst.
Remain EW on Sunny Optical and GoerTek: Sunny is a key variable aperture
component vendor, but it's been well on track to meet the goal of doubling revenue,
implying limited upside surprise. GoerTek could face labor shortages and margin

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Executive Summary
With Largan and Genius's significant outperformance YTD, we believe the iPhone 18
launch is less likely to become a key driver of further stock price movement for them.
Instead, our analysis suggests that the CPO enthusiasm could have already made these
stocks overvalued. We therefore downgrade Largan from OW to  EW and downgrade
Genius from EW  to UW.
Meanwhile, we expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to become the key beneficiaries

amid iPhone new product launches. These stocks haven't rallied materially, and we see
upside potential in them.
We maintain EW ratings on Sunny and GoerTek and expect limited positive surprise from
the iPhone peak season.

### Page 5

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Implications from iPhone 18 Launch for Various Supply
Chain Companies
Camera spec upgrade is a positive earnings driver, but CPO
enthusiasm has made Largan and Genius overvalued
The iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max have applied a variable aperture main camera for the first
time, representing a significant hardware upgrade.  The ASP of  variable aperture lenses will
be higher than it is for traditional lenses. Thus, a rapid shipment increase in 3Q26 could

become a key re-rating catalyst – indeed, it has already prompted sharp re-rating for
Largan, Genius, and Sunny.
Besides this, the launch of Apple's first foldable iPhone will also provide an incremental
demand driver beyond the standard iPhone 18 lineup. According to Nikkei Asia (link),
foldable smartphone orders increased from 8 million units to 10 million units this year,
and if the new product performs well in 4Q2026, it could become a positive catalyst for

Apple supply chain companies.
Beyond variable aperture and foldable, the further development of a new periscope lens
with the key customer is likely to expand  these use of periscope lenses in the future. This
could also become a positive driver for optical lens companies in the supply chain.
1. Largan: iPhone opportunity is real, but recent enthusiasm
over CPO made valuation too aggressive – downgrade to EW
Largan's revenue follows a well-established seasonal pattern tied to Apple's annual iPhone

launch cycle. After shipments declined continuously from March 2026 through June 2026,
we expect a seasonal recovery to begin in 3Q26, with sequential increases highly likely in
the following months. Largan's August 2026 consolidated revenue reached NT$5.012
billion, up 3% month-over-month and its best performance in four months, and the
company expects September's shipment momentum to surpass August's.
Largan is a key supplier of variable aperture lenses for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and the

company could also benefit from foldable and periscope opportunities, we raise our
intrinsic value estimate of the company's traditional business from NT$2,814/share to NT
$3,000/share. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 18.
Besides the Apple lens opportunity, we believe Largan's share performance is also highly
correlated to CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO
opportunity, we undertook the following analysis:

1. We forecast CPO optical engine volume to increase from 0.39mn in 2026 to
7.78mn in 2027,with a further surge to 48.6mn in 2028. Consequently, FAU (Fiber
Array Units – vital optical interconnect components that align multiple optical
fibers with silicon chips, making them critical for Co-Packaged Optics) volume for
CPO also grows from 0.39 to 48.6 mn for the same period of time;
2. We assume ASP of US$100 in 2026-2028 (average market level);
3. We forecast Largan's FAU market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
2027 and 40% in 2028 (previously 20%).
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 30% in 2028, we forecast FAU earnings
of US$4mn in 2027 and US$579 mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU business at 35x 2028e P/E (industry peers are at 40-50x P/E, and
considering the first year of scalable production), it could bring incremental market cap of
US$20.278bn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% rate, we derive a per-share positive
impact of NT$4,100.

We hold a positive view on Largan's CPO/FAU business opportunity because the company
has a superior track record of high precision manufacturing relative to most industry peers
in Taiwan. However, our calculation above suggests that the current share price already
reflects Largan's potential  to attain >30% global market share for FAU products.
Meanwhile, formal production hasn't kicked off yet – it is more likely to start in the

second half of 2027 or even 2028. Therefore, any delay in CPO/FAU development could
result in significant share price fluctuation. Considering current risk-reward, even after
raising our price target 47%, we no longer view Largan as attractive any more in view of
the recent strong share price performance. We set our price target at NT$7,100, within
which NT$3,000 is from its traditional business and NT$4,100 is from CPO/FAU new
business.
2. Genius: Thesis is similar to Largan's –  CPO opportunities

also overpriced, downgrade to UW
Genius, as the second-largest optical lens vendor to Apple, is also well positioned to
benefit from the camera spec upgrade highlighted in the previous section. Considering the
growth opportunities discussed above, we raise our intrinsic value estimate for the

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
existing business from NT$425 to NT$600. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 17.
Similar to Largan, we believe Genius' share price performance is also highly correlated to
CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO opportunity,
we undertook the following analysis:
1. We apply the same FAU volume and ASP assumptions as we do for Largan.
2. We expect Genius to be involved in FAU components, rather than overall

solutions; therefore, we assume components TAM to be 15% of the FAU TAM.
3. We forecast Genius's market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in 2027 and
30% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 25% in 2028, we forecast FAU
component earnings of US$1mn in 2027 and US$54mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU component business at 25x 2028e P/E (lower multiple vs. Largan
because Genius has smaller scale), it could introduce incremental market cap of US

$1,322mn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% discounted rate, we derive a per-share
positive impact of NT$310.

Similar to Largan, our residual income model derives intrinsic value of NT$600/share for
the traditional business, while the TAM analysis suggests CPO/FAU-related opportunities
could be valued at NT$310/share. Adding these together, we raise our price target

materially to NT$910, but that still implies 14% downside from the latest closing price.
3. Sunny: Apple revenue to double in 2026, but positive is
well known
We believe Sunny Optical will benefit from the new iPhone launch because:
1. The company is a key vendor of  variable aperture actuators – a key spec upgrade

### Page 8

M
Idea
8
with higher ASP.
2. Sunny is expanding the product mix from a single category to multiple categories,
which could generate higher revenue from the key client.
3. Sunny is also gaining share in high-end lens products.
These drivers collectively underpin management's guidance of doubling  Apple revenue for
the full-year of 2026.
However, the fundamental performance is in line with management's guidance, so there is
lack of potential positive surprise, in our view. We therefore raise our net income

estimates 29.6% for 2026 and 26.5% for 2027 based on upbeat 1H26 results.
Consequently, we raise our price target to HK$75 but maintain our EW rating –  the strong
growth from Apple has been well expected.
4. Crystal: Prisms, coated filters, and cover lenses to benefit
We expect Crystal Optech to benefit from Apple's new product launch because  the
company is supplying three key products in the supply chain:
1. Its micro prism modules are a key enabling component for periscope (folded-

optics) telephoto cameras in smartphones. The company's prism technology uses a
folded optical path to achieve long-focal-length telephoto capability without
increasing device thickness. In 1H26, the micro prism module business enjoyed
significant volume growth. The iPhone 18 launch will directly extend this demand
into 2H26.
2. Crystal-Optech's coated filter business — which brings micro-nano optical
processing into the IRCF manufacturing process to significantly improve petal

ghosting and chromatic aberration in special imaging scenarios — is another key
segment that benefits from the iPhone 18 launch. Apple's high-end Pro models are
the primary application for this product, so the company is highly likely to
generate rapid revenue and earnings growth.
3. The iPhone 18 Pro models are expected to feature advances in 3D sensing,
including a smaller Dynamic Island as some Face ID components move beneath the
display, which sustains demand for these optical components. Crystal Optech is

well positioned to capture the growth opportunity in iPhone's 3D sensing and
cover lenses.
The company's 2Q26 results were a bit weaker than we expected because of rising costs
from R&D and capacity expansion related to new products. However, we believe this
investment could lead to stronger growth in 2028 and onward. As a result, we trim our
earnings estimates 15.6% for 2026 and 2.9% for 2027 but raise our 2028 estimate 8.2%.
Consequently, we raise our price target  7%, to Rmb32, implying 24% upside from the

current level.
Luxshare: Apple opportunity underappreciated,  resilient
growth intact
We believe Luxshare's Apple opportunity is underappreciated –  some investors worry
about the supply chain relocation. However, the company is one of Apple's most deeply

### Page 9

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 9
embedded manufacturing partners, covering various products and components, as
illustrated in the chart below. The company has  also proved its operational resilience,
consistently registering positive growth in the Apple business.
Exhibit 7: Luxshare: One of the most deeply embedded manufacturing partners for Apple
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
We expect Luxshare to benefit from the latest new product  launch through the following

four key drivers:
1.  Second largest assembler for iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max — the most premium-heavy
iPhone lineup in history. If the  Pro/Pro Max models can take 70%+ of total iPhone
volume, it could mark the highest high-end model contribution over the past
several model cycles. A higher share from high-end models implies better revenue
and margin outlook for Luxshare.
2.   Capturing component opportunity in new  variable aperture actuator supply.

Instead of doing assembly, Lxushare is also playing the role  of supplier of actuator
mechanisms for the variable aperture camera system in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro
Max. This helps Luxshare gain exposure to high-end component business, which
carries higher margin than assembly business.
3. Foldable iPhone (iPhone Ultra) exposure: While Luxshare is not the assembler for
the foldable phone, it has actively developed foldable hinge technology, with R&D

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
focused on high-precision, long-life, large-angle hinge modules for inward-folding
flexible-display devices. The company is also developing ultra-thin haptic motors
tailored for foldable smartphones. When foldable shipments reach a certain scale,
Luxshare is also highly likely to become the second assembler.
4.  Leading AirPods assembly share:  In the AirPods assembly market, we expect
Luxshare to remain the largest supplier, with GoerTek as the second player. The

iPhone 18 launch is accompanied by new AirPods models, and Luxshare's dominant
AirPods assembly position means that it captures the full breadth of Apple's 2H26
product cycle — not just the iPhone.
Despite a significant FX loss, Luxshare posted resilient earnings in 1H26. We nudge our
EPS estimates up 0.2% each for 2026 and 2027 and 2.0% for 2028 to reflect the solid
1H26 results and positive growth outlook in the following 2-3 years. As a result, we also

raise our H-share price target to HK$82/share, implying 34% upside potential.
Lingyi: Peak season to drive strong earnings improvement
Lingyi  provides a broad range of components, including core precision parts, hinge
precision structural components, screen support plates, flanged ultra-thin stainless steel
battery case modules, etc. Following the launch of iPhone 18 Pro, Pro Max, and Apple's
first-ever foldable iPhone, Lingyi is likely to benefit from  higher content per unit on

standard Pro models, and a step-change in content value from the foldable iPhone.
Exhibit 8: Lingyi: Higher content value from new iPhone
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
Lingyi also has good exposure to the iPhone  foldable product, with content value per
foldable device expected by the company to reach US$80–90, including titanium inner
hinges, hinge components, and mid-frame support structures — substantially above the
content value of conventional smartphones.

Considering the positive drivers in 3Q26, we expect Lingyi to achieve significant earnings
improvement on a QoQ basis, which could become a key re-rating catalyst.
We slightly adjust our net income  estimates after 1H26: up 1.6%  in 2026, 0.3% and 1.7% in
2027 and 2028. However, our price targets for both the H- and A-shares are unchanged
because the revision is minor while our long-term RI model assumptions remain

### Page 11

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 11
unchanged.
AAC: Key beneficiary of  iPhone VC application
AAC Tech is one of Apple's key component suppliers, providing a broad portfolio spanning
acoustics, haptics, and thermal management. The iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and iPhone Ultra
launch is a direct, multi-dimensional catalyst across all those business segments.
Within the three segments, we believe thermal management –  Vapor Chamber (VC) Heat
Dissipation – is the most important catalyst. AAC Technologies is a key vendor of VC heat

spreaders in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max. The significant expansion of the VC cooling
area is driven by the iPhone 18 Pro's A20 Pro chip, which debuts on a 2nm process node
with complex WMCM packaging technology to support on-device AI computation, leading
to increased thermal output. Apple first introduced VC liquid cooling in the iPhone 17 Pro
series, with AAC as the primary supplier for that generation as well.
This division has already demonstrated significant growth momentum heading into the

iPhone 18 ramp up. In 1H26, heat dissipation revenue surged approximately 400% YoY, to
RMB1.10 billion, primarily driven by the continued ramp-up in shipments of high-
performance innovative vapor chambers. The company expects VC shipments to exceed
180,000k units in 2026, up from 140,000k+  units in 2025.

Combining the strong growth momentum in thermal management, but weaker-than-
expected performance in the optics segment (all supplied to Android), we raise our
earnings estimates  8.4% for 2026 and 6.0% for 2027. After 1H26 results, we believe
investors are aware of the weakness in optics, while the iPhone launch could help re-rate

the stock. We raise our price target to HK$50 – the stronger 19% increase reflects
earnings upgrades and a higher valuation for the thermal-management business – and
maintain our OW rating.
BYDE: Normal peak season, but lackluster with limited spec
upgrade

### Page 12

M
Idea
12
While Apple is BYDE's key customer, accounting for >50% of total revenue, it is mainly
from the  iPad assembly business. Therefore, this division is less likely to benefit from the
iPhone 18 launch.
The company is also the second-largest iPhone metal casing vendor, supplying precision
structural components including titanium and aluminium alloy chassis. While iPhone 18
launch is the direct driver for this segment, the titanium-to-aluminium downgrade has

become a near-term headwind.  Titanium is a higher-value, higher-margin material for BYDE
while aluminium carries lower ASP and lower gross margin. This change has made BYDE
less likely to benefit from the iPhone 18 launch.
Despite the lackluster outlook, we expect earnings to show sequential improvement in
3Q26, driven by the normal peak season in 3Q26, followed by stronger growth in 4Q26 as
liquid cooling products for AI infrastructure also ramp up. Our earnings estimates and

price target remain unchanged.
GoerTek: Margin might face pressures despite peak season
GoerTek's primary Apple exposure is in smart acoustic complete units (AirPods assembly
and components) and precision components. We believe the  iPhone 18 launch and peak
season ramp in 3Q–4Q will drive higher shipment volumes for these products.
However, GoerTek's transfer of production  to Vietnam — where it has invested
approximately US$1.3 billion through multiple entities — has created a persistent labor

cost headwind. In 2H25, a labor shortage caused by the rapid capacity transfer forced
GoerTek to offer higher wages, triggering material  gross margin pressure  for the smart
acoustic assembly business. Overall gross margins partially recovered to 13.8% in 1Q26
and 15.2% in 2Q26, but the underlying trend of rising wages in Vietnam remains a
structural factor. As GoerTek ramps production for the iPhone 18 peak season in 3Q and
4Q, the higher labor cost base in Vietnam will continue to weigh on assembly margins.

We raise our 2026 and 2027 earnings estimates 14.9% and 10.6%, respectively, mainly
reflecting the better-than-expected gross margin recovery in 1H26. However, we raise our
price target only modestly: 4%. to Rmb27, as persistent labor-cost pressure in Vietnam
could limit further margin expansion and reduce the earnings benefit from higher peak-
season volumes.
Considering the factors discussed above, we believe GoerTek is less likely to benefit from

the new iPhone product launch and peak season volume increase.

Where we could be wrong
While we expect iPhone 18 to be  a positive catalyst in 2H26, we are watching three key
variables on the potential inflection points:
1.  Memory prices: These are a key variable this year. If there is a memory price
correction anytime during 2026, investors are likely to expect an earlier
smartphone shipment and margin recovery, which could reverse the negative

sentiment.
2.  LLMs: We see LLMs as a revolutionary innovation. They could introduce numerous
creative features in smartphone applications and change user habits in the years to
come. If the Edge AI breakthrough arrives earlier than we expect, it could
accelerate the smartphone product cycle and make the recovery earlier.
3. iPhone sales performance: Better-than-expected sell-through of the iPhone 18
Pro, Pro Max and its first foldable model could drive shipment upside, higher

content value and stronger capacity utilization. Conversely, weaker demand could
lead to order cuts after the initial ramp up and reduce the earnings benefits from
key hardware upgrades.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Document 117: 260914_daiwa_aspeed

Source PDF: all report pdf/260914_daiwa_aspeed.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-14
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our update on Aspeed following its upbeat 4Q26 
revenue guidance on 4 September. We believe the strong guidance 
reaffirms our positive view that agentic AI has served as a catalyst for end 
demand for regular servers starting early 2026. Baseboard management 
controller (BMC) shipments for general servers (AI and non-AI together) 
should grow c.50% YoY in 2026. We believe that the new 4Q26 revenue 
guidance implies regular server shipment volume will increase c.50% YoY 
in 2027, hence the 36/34% uplift in our 2027/28E earnings forecasts.

What’s the impact: Solid regular server shipments ahead. Aspeed’s 
latest guidance range for its 4Q26 sales at TWD6.6-6.8bn implies 4Q26 
BMC shipments of c.12m. With this run-rate, total BMC shipments in 2027 
should be near c.50m, much higher than our previous estimate of 36.1m. 
We attribute the ongoing strength to agentic AI and AI-related general-
purpose demand. We forecast Aspeed’s BMC for general servers to 
increase to 27m in 2027E (from 18m in 2026E and 13m in 2025). At the 
same time, we expect BMC for AI servers to increase c.50% to 20m in 
2027E (up from 13m in 2026E and 5m in 2025). To sum up, we now 
forecast BMC shipments of 32.7m in 2026 and 47.6m in 2027 (+46% YoY 
in 2027). Based on our AI-related general server assumptions (excluding 
non-AI general servers), we expect the CPU:XPU ratio to rise to 1:1.3 in 
2027E (from 1:2.6 in 2026E). We expect a rising CPU:XPU ratio to become 
a key growth driver and share-price catalyst in the years after 2027. Due to 
the solid end demand, management is considering adjusting its pricing 
again from November to factor in the additional packaging cost, leading to 
our higher gross margin assumptions from 4Q26.

New growth opportunities. Following our visits to Aspeed’s booth at 
Computex 2026 and the OCP APAC conference, we believe Aspeed will 
continually maximise the dollar content per server board. Compared with 
last year’s version (embedded software-defined FPGA), Aspeed is 
integrating a Lattice FPGA into its next-generation Bridge IC (SMC; 
AST1840), which should support higher ASPs for its products.

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 9-36% to factor in our 
higher BMC shipment assumptions. We lift our 12M TP to TWD26,660 
(from TWD19,800), based on an unchanged PER of 66x (high end of its 
past-3-year PER range of 21-78x), applied to our 1-year forward EPS 
forecast. Our 2025-28E EPS CAGR of 83% makes our target PER 
undemanding, given the resulting PEG ratio of 0.8. Key downside risk: 
weak AI server demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 6-26% higher than the consensus, 
likely due to our bullish view on Aspeed’s ASP and shipment story.

14 September 2026

Promising outlook on agentic AI

➢ Rising CPU:XPU ratio is becoming a key growth driver for Aspeed… 
➢ … which indicates solid regular server demand despite supply hiccups 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating and raising 12-month TP to TWD26,660
